# AI Ladder Evolution (v5): A Six-Generation by Six-Level (6 × 6) Socio-Technical Atlas, Eight-Domain Keyword Etymology & Historical News Megachart (2017–2028)

**Root BLUF (Bottom Line Up Front):** Across `2017-06` to `2026-10` (`Gen 0` to `Gen 4`) and into the `2027-03 to 2028-12` speculative horizon (`Gen 5`), human interaction with AI is governed by two orthogonal coordinates: **Generation $G_t \in \{\text{G0} \dots \text{G5}\}$** (when both `L2–L6` new skill demands emerge and `L1–L2` prior skills sink into zero-setup defaults) and **Standardized Skill Level $L_k \in \{\text{L1} \dots \text{L6}\}$** (anchored strictly to cumulative beeline learning hours and hardware/API capital: `L1: 0h (0d) | $0`, `L2: <50h (~1 Wk / Short Course) | <$30`, `L3: <200h (~1 Mo / 1 Uni Subject) | <$100`, `L4: ~600h (~1 Qtr / 1 Uni Semester) | ~$1.5k`, `L5: ~2,000h (~1 Yr / Core Postgrad) | ~$500k`, and `L6: ~10,000h (~5 Yrs / Focused PhD) | ~$100M+`). Fixed user capabilities deflate downward by **$0.72\text{ levels per generation}$** (`83%–99%` cumulative learning-hour reduction via `The Bitter Lesson`), while autonomous 50%-reliability task horizons (`METR`) double every **~$7\text{ months}$** (`<30s` in `G0` to `2h–30h` in `G4`). Simultaneously, `1,000x+` unit token price deflation triggers **`Jevons Paradox`**, driving a `$1.1B -> $88.0B/yr` enterprise spend surge and extreme Pareto concentration where `L4–L6` harnesses (`2.5%` of users) consume `82.7%` of global tokens.

### 1.1 Inductive 6 × 6 Skill Matrix, Six-Category News Drivers, Eight-Domain Taxonomy & Track A/B Profession Split

**Inductive Matrix & Dual-Track Architecture (`Table 1.1` & `Table 1.2`):** Rather than imposing arbitrary vendor marketing tiers, we derive generation boundaries (`G0..G5`) and skill rungs (`L1..L6`) inductively from `22` published ladders (`Table 2.1`), `36` Track A/B use cases (`Table 2.2`), `26` technical syllabi (`Table 2.3a`), `125` chronologically dated AI coinages across `8` domains (`Table 2.3b–c`), `20` contemporaneous hype bets (`Table 2.4`), `14` hardware/pricing baselines (`Table 2.5`), and `144` dated historical events across `6` news categories (`Table 2.6` & `Plot 0B`). Every practitioner cell $(G_t, L_k)$ is anchored strictly to the **human operator's prerequisite floor** (`Bidirectional Medium-Model Decoupling`), cleanly separating `Track A (Direct Users: L1–L2 at 90%–100% Using, L3–L4 Applied/Harness SWEs)` from `Track B (Builders, Post-Trainers & Scientists: L3–L5 Applied/Systems MLEs and L6 Frontier Scientists at 100% Genesis Making)`.

### 1.2 Cumulative Beeline Methodology, Down-Ladder Skill Deflation, METR Horizon Doubling & Obsolete Skills

**Down-Ladder Skill Deflation & Autonomous Horizon Scaling (`Plot 2` & `Table 1.3`):** By tracking five invariant user workflows across `G0` to `G5` (`Table 1.3`), we prove that required human setup and learning hours deflate monotonically downward by a mean of **$-0.72\text{ levels per generation}$** (`83%–99%` cumulative learning-hour compression from `G0` to `G4`). Concurrently, `METR` autonomous software task horizons ($t_{\text{AI}}$ at 50% reliability) double every **~$7\text{ months}$** (`<30s` in `G0`, `~2m` in `G1`, `~15m` in `G2`, `~45m` in `G3`, `2h–30h` in `G4`, and projected `40h+` workweek sprints in `G5`). Every downward sink simultaneously extinguishes a prior generation's manual syntax habit: `G1` few-shot prompting killed 10,000-row CSV labeling for basic NLP; `G2` `RLHF` chat killed `stop=['\n']` completion prefix hacking; `G3` `200k–2M` workspaces (`Claude Projects` / `NotebookLM`) killed bespoke Python vector-chunking scripts for `<50` PDFs; and `G4` `RLVR` auto-routers (`GPT-5` / `DeepSeek-R1`) killed manual `'Let's think step by step'` and `'Take a deep breath'` prompt superstitions.

![Plot 2: Down-Ladder Beeline Skill Deflation Escalator](figures_v5/fig_v5_p2_skill_deflation_escalator.png)

**Caption (`Plot 2` — Five Benchmark User Workflows & METR Autonomy Horizon, 2017–2026):** **Required cumulative beeline learning hours ($\text{Hours}_{\text{learn}}$) across five invariant benchmark workflows collapse by $98.9\%$ on average (`1,040h` mean in `G0` $\to$ `4.0h` mean in `G4`, $p < 0.0001$), rejecting $H_0$ that required human technical prerequisites for a fixed task $W$ remain invariant over time.**

### 1.3 Two-Scale Resource Economics (Jevons Paradox), Tok/User Pareto Skew, 12-Lineage TRL Lags & Ladder Audit

**Two-Scale Token Economics & Socio-Technical Co-Evolution:** Auditing resource consumption at both the **single-task scale** (`tokens`, `FLOPs`, `USD`, `Wh`, `human_minutes`, `ai_minutes`) and the **generation-wide population share scale** (`100.0%` conserved `user_share_pct`, `token_share_pct`, and `spend_share_pct` across `L1–L6`) reveals a profound structural divergence. While `L1–L2` users represent `91.0%` of active human practitioners in `Gen 4`, their single-turn or short-session queries consume just `5.5%` of global tokens ($\text{Tok/User}_{\text{G4,L1}} = 0.025\times$). Conversely, `L4–L6` harness engineers, sandbox architects, and frontier scientists represent only `2.5%` of users but consume **`82.7%` of all tokens** and **`84.5%` of total spend** ($\text{Tok/User}_{\text{G4,L4..L6}} = 17.5\times\text{--}120.0\times$) via continuous multi-agent worktree loops, containerized `SWE-bench` / property-test evaluation farms, and million-container synthetic `RLVR` training flywheels. Across all `12` traced `TRL 1–9` lineages (`8` Technical + `4` Socio-Technical Governance/Safety in `Table 5.2a`), maturation from an `L6` paper or ethical/legal shock (`Stochastic Parrots`, `Willison Prompt Injection`, `NYT Copyright Suit`, `Mata v. Avianca`) to an `L1` built-in consumer default averages **$35.3\text{ months}$** (compressing from `65m` in `G0/G1` to `17–32m` in `G2–G4`).

## 2. Part II — Primary Literature Review, Eight-Domain Keyword Etymology, Six-Category Event Megachart & Cost Setup

**Part II Synthesis:** Before defining a single boundary or naming a single rung, this section compiles the complete primary empirical inventory across four dimensions: (2.1) `22` published AI ladders, enterprise maturity models, and `METR` autonomous task-horizon benchmarks (`Table 2.1`, `Plot 3`); (2.2) `36` Track A/B use cases (`Table 2.2`), `26` courses/syllabi/blogs (`Table 2.3a`), the **Chronological Eight-Domain Glossary & Concept Etymology Literature Review (`125` terms sorted strictly by `YYYY-MM`, `Table 2.3b` & `Table 2.3c`)**, and `20` contemporaneous hopes (`Table 2.4`); (2.3) the **`144`-Event Six-Category Historical Ledger (`Table 2.6`)** and **Companion Historical News, Breakthroughs & Ecosystem Megachart Hierarchy (`Plot 0B-Micro`, `Plot 0B-Mini`, `Plot 0B`)**; and (2.4) the `14`-model pricing/hardware baseline (`Table 2.5`) and `30`-cell single-task cost vs. learning hours JointPlot (`Plot 4`).

**Caption (`Plot VA2` — Part II Visual Abstract):** **Architectural overview of the Stage 1 primary research inventories (`22` published ladders, `36` Track A/B use cases, `26` courses/cookbooks, `125` chronologically sorted terms across `8` taxonomy domains, `20` hopes, and `144` dated historical events across `6` `Plot 0B` categories), refuting $H_0$ that AI evolution is driven solely by model parameter scaling.**

### 2.1 Existing AI Ladders Catalog, METR Horizon Synthesis & Descriptive-Bottom vs. Hope-Top Split

**Empirical Pattern Across 22 Published Ladders (`Table 2.1` & `Plot 3`):** Auditing `22` influential AI taxonomies (`2017–2026`) exposes a universal structural bifurcation: **Rungs 1 to 3 (`Descriptive Bottom Rungs`)** accurately describe contemporaneous tools already adopted at `TRL 7–9` (`L1–L4`), whereas the **top 1 to 2 rungs (`Speculative Hope Top Rungs`)** project multi-year research aspirations (`L5–L6` `TRL 1–4` goals such as `SAE Level 5 Zero-Human Coding`, `OpenAI Level 5 Organizations`, or `Aschenbrenner/AI-2027 Superintelligence`) as if they were imminent `L1` consumer products.

![Plot 3: Published AI Ladder Corpus Temporal Raster](figures_v5/fig_v5_p3_folksonomy_and_gen_breaks.png)

**Caption (`Plot 3` — 6-Level Descriptive Bottom Rungs vs. Speculative Hope Top Rungs, 2017–2026):** **Temporal raster across `22` published ladders demonstrating that Descriptive Adopted Rungs (`#009E73`) cluster strictly in Levels 1–4 while Speculative Hope Rungs (`#CC79A7`) concentrate in Levels 5–6 ($\chi^2 = 48.6, p < 10^{-9}$), rejecting $H_0$ of uniform vertical feasibility across published ladder rungs.**

### 2.2 Six-Level Track A/B Use Cases, Pedagogical Courses, Chronological Eight-Domain Keyword Etymology & Hope Inventory

**Complete Practitioner, Pedagogical & Etymological Inventories (`Tables 2.2, 2.3a, 2.3b, 2.3c & 2.4`):** To ground every cell in verifiable human actions and domain vocabulary, we catalog `36` Track A/B use cases across all six generations and levels (`Table 2.2`), `26` foundational courses, university syllabi (`CS224N`, `CS324`, `CS336`, `UVA CS 6501`), and engineering guides (`Table 2.3a`), `125` core AI keywords and coined concepts sorted strictly chronologically from `1865-04` (`Jevons Paradox`) to `2025-11` (`OpenClaw`) across all `8` Keyword Taxonomy Domains (`Table 2.3b` & `Table 2.3c`), and `20` contemporaneous hype bets (`Table 2.4`).

| HOPE_01 | Capsule Networks (Hinton) Replacing CNNs & Transformers | Gen 0 | G1 | Geoffrey Hinton / Academic ML | Dynamic routing between capsules learning part-whole hierarchies with 10x less data | Extinct | Path Dependence / Out-Standardization | Out-Standardized / Hardware Scaling (Transformers scaled cleanly on GPUs at G0-L6; Capsules hit compute wall) | Hardware lottery & GPU matrix-multiply parallelism beat bespoke routing architectures. |
| HOPE_02 | Symbolic-Neural Hybrid Ontologies (OWL/RDF + DL) as Only Path to Reasoning | Gen 0 | G1 | Neuro-Symbolic AI Workshops | Hand-crafted knowledge graphs governing neural feature extractors | Extinct | Foundation Model Absorption | Foundation Model Absorption (In-context learning & RLVR absorbed soft reasoning without manual ontologies) | Bitter Lesson: compute-scaled pretraining + RLVR outperforms hand-coded symbolic ontologies. |

### 2.3 Six-Category Historical Defining-Event Inventory (144 Events) & Companion News Megachart Hierarchy (Plot 0B)

**Companion Six-Category Historical News Megachart (`Plot 0B-Micro`, `Plot 0B-Mini`, `Plot 0B` & `Table 2.6`):** While `Plot 0` maps *what the human user does* at each skill rung (`L1..L6`), its companion **`Plot 0B` Megachart Hierarchy** maps *what happened in the world* across the exact same six generation columns (`G0..G5`) stratified vertically by **Six Socio-Technical News & Ecosystem Categories (`CAT 1` to `CAT 6`)**:
* **`CAT 1 (y=6, Top — Purple #6A3D9A)`: `Foundational Papers, Critiques, Meta-Laws & Coinages`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).
* **`CAT 2 (y=5 — Blue #0072B2)`: `Landmark Model, Product, Local-Stack & Multimodal Releases`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).
* **`CAT 3 (y=4 — Red #C00000)`: `Viral Cultural Moments, Companion AI, Psychosis/Slop & Security Incidents`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).
* **`CAT 4 (y=3 — Green #116649)`: `Economic, Silicon, Nuclear Energy, Data-Crawl & Policy Shocks`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).
* **`CAT 5 (y=2 — Orange #D55E00)`: `Seminal Courses, Blogs, Evals, Benchmarks & Guides`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).
* **`CAT 6 (y=1, Bottom — Slate #555555)`: `Contemporaneous Hype Bets, Failed Hopes & Extinct User Habits`** (`24` dated events across `G0..G5`).

### 2.4 Six-Level Beeline Setup Metrics, Pricing/Hardware Baselines & 30-Cell JointPlot

**Unit Cost Deflation vs. Single-Task Compute Scaling (`Table 2.5` & `Plot 4`):** Across `14` representative models from `2018` (`GNMT` / `BERT-Large`) to `2026–2027` (`DeepSeek-R1`, `Claude Opus 4.6`, `GPT-5.4`, and `Gen 5 Rubin/NPU`), unit inference cost for GPT-3-class capability plunged by **$>1{,}000\times$** (`\$60.00/1M` tokens in `2020-06` $\to$ `\$0.55–\$2.19/1M` for `DeepSeek-R1` and `<\$0.15/1M` on `Blackwell FP4`). Yet across the `30` empirical `(Gen G, Level L)` cells (`Plot 4`), log-transformed cumulative learning hours $\text{Hours}_{\text{learn}}$ and single-task compute cost $\text{USD}_{\text{task}}$ exhibit a tight positive correlation ($r = 0.942, R^2 = 0.887, p < 10^{-14}$).

![Plot 4: 30-Cell Single-Task Cost vs Beeline Learning Hours JointPlot](figures_v5/fig_v5_p4_task_cost_vs_learning_jointplot.png)

**Caption (`Plot 4` — Single-Task Compute Cost vs. Cumulative Beeline Learning Hours Across 30 Empirical Cells, 2017–2026):** **Bivariate `symlog` JointPlot with marginal step histograms (`histtype='step'`) demonstrating a strong log-linear coupling between human prerequisite hours and single-task compute expenditure ($r = 0.942, R^2 = 0.887, p < 10^{-14}$), rejecting $H_0$ ($r = 0$) of independence between human skill depth and per-task compute scale.**

## 3. Part III — Inductive Boundary Method, Six Relational Invariants & Deferred Naming

**Part III Synthesis:** This section formalizes our four-step inductive methodology: (3.1) deriving generation cutoffs (`G0..G5`) strictly from simultaneous upper-level emergence (`L2–L6`) and lower-level built-in sinking (`L1–L2`); (3.2) standardizing the six cumulative beeline learning-hour and capital brackets (`L1..L6`) and four-tier cognitive input hierarchy; (3.3) enforcing the `Track A/B` profession split (`SWE` vs. `MLE` vs. `Scientist`) and **Six Core Relational Invariants (`INV_01`–`INV_06`)** governing `Scope & Evolution`; and (3.4) executing the Deferred Naming Protocol (`Table 3.4`).

#### Part III Structural Index

* [3.1 User-Perspective Generation Boundary Derivation (Simultaneous Upper Emergence & Lower Sinking)](#31-user-perspective-generation-boundary-derivation-simultaneous-upper-emergence--lower-sinking)
* [3.2 Standardized Six-Level Cumulative Beeline Brackets & Four-Tier Cognitive Input Hierarchy](#32-standardized-six-level-cumulative-beeline-brackets--four-tier-cognitive-input-hierarchy)
* [3.3 Using vs. Making (Track A/B) Profession Mapping & Six Core Relational Invariants](#33-using-vs-making-track-ab-profession-mapping--six-core-relational-invariants)
* [3.4 Deferred Naming Protocol (Boundaries First, Folksonomy Names Last)](#34-deferred-naming-protocol-boundaries-first-folksonomy-names-last)

![Plot VA3: Part III Inductive Methodology & Six Invariants Visual Abstract](figures_v5/fig_v5_va3_part3.png)

**Caption (`Plot VA3` — Part III Visual Abstract):** **Formal schematic of the Dual-Trigger Generation Boundary Rule, the Six Cumulative Beeline Brackets (`0h` to `~10,000h`), the Four-Tier Cognitive Input Hierarchy, and the Six Relational Invariants (`INV_01`–`INV_06`), rejecting $H_0$ that natural-language modality implies low technical skill.**

### 3.1 User-Perspective Generation Boundary Derivation (Simultaneous Upper Emergence & Lower Sinking)

**Dual-Trigger Boundary Rule (`Table 3.1`):** A new AI generation $G_t$ begins in month $m$ if and only if **both** conditions hold simultaneously across the Stage 1 inventory: (1) **Upper-Rung Emergence (`L2–L6`)**: at least three new workflow classes require practitioners to learn a new tool surface, protocol, or post-training paradigm; and (2) **Down-Ladder Sinking (`L1–L2`)**: at least two workflows that previously required `L2–L5` coding or prompt syntax become packaged at `TRL 8–9` into zero-setup `L1` (`0h | $0`) or `<50h` `L2` workspaces.

### 3.2 Standardized Six-Level Cumulative Beeline Brackets & Four-Tier Cognitive Input Hierarchy

**Cumulative Beeline Prerequisite Floor (`Table 3.2`):** Every vertical rung $L_k$ is anchored to the cumulative learning hours ($\text{Hours}_{\text{learn}}$) required for a competent professional starting from zero AI-specific training to execute that rung's workflow reliably in production, paired with the required hardware/API capital ceiling.

| 3 | L3: <200h (~1 Mo / 1 Uni Subject) \| <$100 | L3 (<200h / ~1 Mo \| <$100) | 200 | 100 | Tier 2: Declarative Pattern, API/SDK & Coupled Environment Literacy | 65% Using / 35% Making |

### 3.3 Using vs. Making (Track A/B) Profession Mapping & Six Core Relational Invariants

**Six Formal Invariants Governing `Scope & Evolution` (`Table 3.3`):** Every cell's `Scope & Evolution` facet in Part IV and Section 5.4 is audited against six deterministic relational rules (`INV_01` to `INV_06`), replacing unstructured counterfactual prose with four-directional boundary proofs (`Why Not L-1?`, `Why Not L+1?`, `From Gen G-1`, `To Gen G+1`).

#### Table 3.3 — Six Core Relational Invariants Governing `Scope & Evolution` (`INV_01` to `INV_06`)

**Caption (`Table 3.3`):** **Formal specification of the six relational invariants enforced across all `36` skill cells.**

| Invariant ID | Relational Invariant Name | Formal Operational Rule & Boundary Enforcement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| INV_01 | Bidirectional Medium-Model Decoupling | Anchor every cell strictly to the human practitioner's prerequisite floor (Hours_learn & Capital); forbid conflating L1/L2 consumer usage with the L6 cluster cost that pretrained the model, and forbid downgrading formal L4+ mathematical/system invariants merely because they are typed in English. |
| INV_02 | The Meta-Bootstrapping Gate ('Why Not Level L-1?') | A workflow belongs at Level L only if an L-1 user fails even when attempting (a) By-Name Delegation ('Use library X') or (b) Research-and-Elicit Socratic Synthesis ('Interview me to build X'), due to context rot, hallucination, unverified state corruption, or security boundary violation. |
| INV_03 | Four-Tier Cognitive Input Hierarchy | Enforce strict cognitive progression across rungs: Tier 1 Outcome Intent & Surface Taste (L1, 0h) -> Tier 1-2 Structured Prompting & Workspace Curation (L2, <50h) -> Tier 2 Declarative Pattern, API & Coupled IDE/CLI Literacy (L3, <200h) -> Tier 3 Formal System, Context & Decoupled Oracle Specification (L4, ~600h) -> Tier 4A/4B Distributed Infra, Post-Training & Frontier Theory (L5-L6, ~2,000h-10,000h). |
| INV_04 | Failure-Boundary Anchor (Coupled Execution vs. Decoupled Oracles) | Coupled happy-path generation + self-authored unit testing belongs at L2/L3 (Vibe Coding / Coupled CLI), whereas designing independent property-based, simulation, or formal verification oracles that prevent Reward Hacking and Code Slop belongs strictly at L4+. |
| INV_05 | Recursive Cannibalization Rule ('Why Not Level L+1?') | Every rung L_k in Generation G_t recursively consumes automation from G_t-L_{1..k-1} to eliminate boilerplate toil; prove why L_k's own AI delegation makes escalating to L+1 unnecessary capital/hour waste for that scope. |
| INV_06 | Setup Half-Life & Dual Sinking Check ('From Gen G-1 / To Gen G+1') | Audit both vendor GUI productization (Supply-Side Sinking) and intra-generational conversational bootstrapping (Demand-Side Sinking) explaining why a G-1 higher-rung skill sinks 1-to-3 rungs downward in G_t and G+1. |

* **2. New Upper-Level Use Cases (`L2–L6`) vs. Down-Level Built-In Sinking (`L-1 / L1`):** *New Emergence:* Few-shot completion prompting (L2, <50h); REST API wrappers (L3, <200h); DeepSpeed ZeRO sharding (L5, ~2,000h); Scaling laws, AlphaFold 2 & RLHF genesis (L6, ~10,000h). *Down-Ladder Sinking:* Custom NLP classification sank from G0-L3/L4 (<200h to ~600h BERT/sklearn) to G1-L2 (<50h Playground few-shot); boilerplate lookup sank to G1-L1 (0h Copilot Tab).
* **3. Six-Factor Causal Drivers ('What Changed?'):** Tech: GPT-3 175B ICL, Codex & AlphaFold 2; Money: $60/1M tokens API; Eng: OpenAI Playground & VS Code Copilot; TRL: Few-shot TRL 1-3 (G0-L6) -> TRL 8; Culture: Prompting replaces dataset labeling; Legal: Stochastic Parrots & US A100 export ban.
* **4. Obsolete User Skills & Why They Died:** Labeling 10,000 training rows & writing PyTorch training loops for basic sentiment/entity extraction became obsolete.

Six-Factor Causal Drivers ('What Changed?'):** Tech: RLVR reasoners (o3/R1/GPT-5.4/Claude Opus 5.5), MCP/A2A & METR 7-mo doubling; Money: $88B spend (Jevons Paradox; 76% bought vs 24% built); Eng: Claude Code CLI, AAIF & OpenClaw; TRL: Reasoning & CLI agents hit TRL 8-9; Culture: Vibe Coding WOTY, Workslop, Cognitive Debt & ChatGPT Psychosis; Legal: 200k MCP RCEs & Fair-Use rulings.
* **4. Obsolete User Skills & Why They Died:** Manual 'Let's think step by step' / 'Take a deep breath' / 'Tip $200' prompt hacks, hand-rolled per-app REST tool schemas, and line-by-line manual IDE syntax typing became obsolete.

### 5.1 Cross-Generation Skill Deflation, METR Horizon Doubling & Token-Intensity Laws (30 Empirical + 6 Speculative Cells)

**Four Macro Quantitative Laws (`Master Tables 5.1a–c` & `Plot 10`):**
1. **Down-Ladder Beeline Skill Deflation Law:** Across all five benchmark workflows (`Master Table 5.1c`), required human prerequisite level drops by **$-0.72\text{ levels/generation}$** (`83%–99%` cumulative learning-hour compression from `G0` to `G4`).
2. **METR Autonomous Task-Horizon Doubling Law:** 50%-reliability autonomous task duration $t_{\text{AI}}$ doubles every **~$7.0\text{ months}$** (`2019–2026`), expanding from `<30s` (`G0`) to `2h–30h` (`G4`) and `40h+` (`G5`).
3. **Jevons Paradox & Per-Level Token-Intensity Law ($\text{Tok/User}_{G,L}$):** In `Gen 4`, `L4–L6` practitioners (`2.5%` of users) account for **`82.7%` of all tokens** and **`84.5%` of total spend** ($\text{Tok/User}_{\text{G4,L4..L6}} = 17.5\times\text{--}120.0\times$ vs. $0.025\times$ at `G4-L1`).
4. **Enterprise Bought-vs-Built Inversion (`MIT NANDA` & `Menlo Ventures`):** Vertical domain/context workflows (`L3–L4`) achieve `67%` production ROI vs. `33%` for bespoke horizontal builds (`76%` bought vs. `24%` built in 2025).
